- **Executor (Broad)**: Mid-tier execution agent for well-specified work spanning multiple components, systems, or files — code, configuration, IaC, documentation, or specs. ALWAYS invoke for cross-cutting refactors, migrations, bulk rewrites, and multi-file changes that need sustained reasoning across a large surface. Do not use for tightly-scoped single-component edits (use Executor (Focused)) or for unspecified work that needs a plan first (use the Plan agent). Keywords: implement, refactor, migrate, restructure, bulk edit, multi-component, cross-cutting, large change. Do not use for cross-cutting or multi-component work (use Executor (Broad)) or unscoped work that needs a plan first (use the Plan agent). - **explore**: Fast exploration of src/graphcrew/ modules without reading tests or examples ([Atiqul-Islam/graphcrew](https://github.com/Atiqul-Islam/graphcrew/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/explore.md))
- **Explore**: Finds where code lives in this project by reading its maps instead of scanning the repository. Use for any "where is X", "which file handles Y", or "what touches Z" question, and before any change that needs an attachment point. - **explore**: Locate files and code relevant to a question and report where they are and what they do. Use for "find", "where is", "which file handles" style questions - never for a task that needs to read a whole file's content into the caller's own context. ([bradnovation/headquarters](https://github.com/bradnovation/headquarters/blob/HEAD/examples/settings/agents/explore.md))
- **explore**: Fast codebase exploration — find files, search code, understand structure. Read-only, no modifications. ([brunossilveira/dotfiles](https://github.com/brunossilveira/dotfiles/blob/HEAD/.pi/agent/agents/explore.md))
- **Explore**: Fast, read-only codebase search on Haiku. Use for finding files by pattern, searching code for keywords, tracing a JS symbol through apex-lsp, or answering where/how questions with path:line evidence. The caller names a thoroughness level (quick, medium or very thorough). Overrides Claude Code's built-in Explore, which inherits the session's model. Never edits, no browser. ([brycejmurrin/f1-game](https://github.com/brycejmurrin/f1-game/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/Explore.md))
- **Explore**: Read-only repository search and fan-out exploration for Wingman—locating specs, tracing source/provenance fields, mapping conventions, and surveying many files. Shadows built-in Explore so broad searches use Haiku instead of the session model. Use when only a concise conclusion and path references are needed. ([bryding/wingman-agents](https://github.com/bryding/wingman-agents/blob/HEAD/plugins/wingman/agents/Explore.md))
- **Explore**: Fast read-only search agent for locating code. Use it to find files by pattern (eg. "src/components/**/*.tsx"), grep for symbols or keywords (eg. "API endpoints"), or answer "where is X defined / which files reference Y." Do NOT use it for code review, design-doc auditing, cross-file consistency checks, or open-ended analysis — it reads excerpts rather than whole files and will miss content past its read window. When calling, specify search breadth: "quick" for a single targeted lookup, "medium" for moderate exploration, or "very thorough" to search across multiple locations and naming conven… ([Cohey0727/CodingAgentTools](https://github.com/Cohey0727/CodingAgentTools/blob/HEAD/pi/agents/Explore.md))
- **Explore**: Read-only broad fan-out search. Use it for sweeps across many files, directories, or naming conventions where only the conclusion matters, not the file contents. Reads excerpts rather than whole files, so it locates code but does not review or audit it. State the breadth you need, e.g. "moderate" or "very thorough". ([cordwainersmith/Claudoscope](https://github.com/cordwainersmith/Claudoscope/blob/HEAD/Claudoscope/Resources/RoutingStack/agents/Explore.md))

- **Explore**: Fast read-only codebase search and analysis. Use for finding files, call sites and patterns across the repo, and summarising how something works, instead of reading many files in the main conversation. ([DeepanshuOP/FillScore](https://github.com/DeepanshuOP/FillScore/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/Explore.md))
- **explore**: Read-only codebase reconnaissance for FairwayIQ. Use before planning any phase to map relevant files, types, and call sites. Returns a summary only, never edits. ([dhruvsood12/FairwayIQ](https://github.com/dhruvsood12/FairwayIQ/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/explore.md))
- **explore**: Deeply analyzes existing codebase features by tracing execution paths, mapping architecture layers, understanding patterns and abstractions, and documenting dependencies. Read-only investigation in isolated context. Vendored from `feature-dev:code-explorer`; adapted for team-setup voice, discipline awareness (TDD/DRY/SOLID + ADR refs), and structured findings format. Useful for unfamiliar codebase deep-dives where main-context exploration would clutter the conversation. ([dlawrynowicz/claude-setup](https://github.com/dlawrynowicz/claude-setup/blob/HEAD/agents/explore.md))
- **Explore**: Agente de exploración read-only, rápido y barato, para buscar y entender código. Override del built-in Explore para clavarlo en Haiku (el built-in hereda el modelo de la sesión — con un main loop en tier alto, explorar código costaría tier alto). Úsalo para descubrir archivos, buscar símbolos/patrones y responder "¿dónde está X / cómo se conecta Y?" sin modificar nada. ([eKoh951/renta-facil-app](https://github.com/eKoh951/renta-facil-app/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/Explore.md))
- **Explore**: Fast, read-only search and analysis of the codebase. Use for file discovery, code search, and codebase exploration. ([elranu/dogwalk](https://github.com/elranu/dogwalk/blob/HEAD/plugins/ranu/agents/explore.md) · 2 copies)
- **Explore**: Read-only search agent for broad fan-out searches — when answering means sweeping many files, directories, or naming conventions and you only need the conclusion, not the file dumps. It reads excerpts rather than whole files, so it locates code; it doesn't review or audit it. Specify search breadth — "medium" for moderate exploration, "very thorough" for multiple locations and naming conventions. ([ercgn/claude-orchestrator](https://github.com/ercgn/claude-orchestrator/blob/HEAD/agents/Explore.md))
- **explore**: Read-only investigation - trace how something works, find where behaviour lives, compare approaches, or research a library or API. Fast; returns a sourced brief with file:line anchors. Cannot edit files or run commands. Use before any non-trivial change and for "how / why / where" questions. ([frederikgnie/lost-mary](https://github.com/frederikgnie/lost-mary/blob/HEAD/agents/explore.md))
